Title: Breve, e succinta relatione del viaggio nel regno di Congo nell'Africa meridionale, fatto dal P. Girolamo Merolla da Sorrento, sacerdote cappuccino, missionario apostolico
Creator: Girolamo Merolla da Sorrento, Capuchin; written up and put in narrative form by Angelo Piccardo da Napoli
Publisher: Napoli: per Francesco Mollo, 1692
Publication Date: 1692
Rights: Public Domain (published 1692, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item breueesuccintare00mero (https://archive.org/details/breueesuccintare00mero), the item's own breueesuccintare00mero_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1692. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 25a5a164ded01db54c2015af487a39e6d7da857ab114394b8803e68a43cbbdbe.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. John Carter Brown Library scan; usable OCR with the long s read as f and some broken lines at the page edges. Two parts, with woodcut figures.
Content note: A Capuchin's account of his mission in Soyo and Kongo in the 1680s: the journey, the land, the customs of the people, the friars' struggle against what they called superstition, and the politics of Soyo and the divided kingdom in the generation before the Antonian movement.
